```
using Microsoft.Xna.Framework;

namespace Terraria.WorldBuilding;

public abstract class GenSearch : GenBase
{
	public static Point NOT_FOUND = new Point(int.MaxValue, int.MaxValue);

	private bool _requireAll = true;

	private GenCondition[] _conditions;

	public GenSearch Conditions(params GenCondition[] conditions)
	{
		_conditions = conditions;
		return this;
	}

	public abstract Point Find(Point origin);

	protected bool Check(int x, int y)
	{
		for (int i = 0; i < _conditions.Length; i++)
		{
			if (_requireAll ^ _conditions[i].IsValid(x, y))
			{
				return !_requireAll;
			}
		}
		return _requireAll;
	}

	public GenSearch RequireAll(bool mode)
	{
		_requireAll = mode;
		return this;
	}
}
```

```
namespace Terraria.WorldBuilding;

public abstract class GenCondition : GenBase
{
	private enum AreaType
	{
		And,
		Or,
		None
	}

	private bool InvertResults;

	private int _width;

	private int _height;

	private AreaType _areaType = AreaType.None;

	public bool IsValid(int x, int y)
	{
		switch (_areaType)
		{
		case AreaType.None:
			return CheckValidity(x, y) ^ InvertResults;
		case AreaType.And:
		{
			for (int k = x; k < x + _width; k++)
			{
				for (int l = y; l < y + _height; l++)
				{
					if (!CheckValidity(k, l))
					{
						return InvertResults;
					}
				}
			}
			return !InvertResults;
		}
		case AreaType.Or:
		{
			for (int i = x; i < x + _width; i++)
			{
				for (int j = y; j < y + _height; j++)
				{
					if (CheckValidity(i, j))
					{
						return !InvertResults;
					}
				}
			}
			return InvertResults;
		}
		default:
			return true;
		}
	}

	public GenCondition Not()
	{
		InvertResults = !InvertResults;
		return this;
	}

	public GenCondition AreaOr(int width, int height)
	{
		_areaType = AreaType.Or;
		_width = width;
		_height = height;
		return this;
	}

	public GenCondition AreaAnd(int width, int height)
	{
		_areaType = AreaType.And;
		_width = width;
		_height = height;
		return this;
	}

	protected abstract bool CheckValidity(int x, int y);
}
```

````
Actually the key point from Terraria modding knowledge: TileObjectData placement anchor is at the bottom. `WorldGen.PlaceObject(x, y, type, style)` places a TileObject anchored so that (x, y) is the position of the **bottom-left (or bottom-center)** tile... Precisely: for TileObjectData, `Position` covers from `(x - anchorX, y - height + 1)` to `(x + width - anchorX - 1, y)`. So y is the BOTTOM row.
````

```
					{
						if (!tile.active())
						{
							tile.active(active: true);
							tile.type = (ushort)bestType;
						}
						tile.slope(0);
						tile.halfBrick(halfBrick: false);
						WorldGen.TileFrame(i, j, resetFrame: true);
						if (flag)
						{
							break;
						}
						flag = true;
					}
					while (!tile.active());
					tile.wire(wire: true);
					if (Main.tileSolid[tile.type])
					{
						tile.actuator(actuator: true);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					tile.ClearTile();
				}
			}
		}
		int num = position.X + 1;
		int num2 = position.Y - requiredHeight + 1;
		int num3 = 3;
		int num4 = num - num3;
		int num5 = num2 - num3;
		int num6 = num + num3 - 1;
		int num7 = num2 + num3 - 1;
		for (int k = num4; k <= num6; k++)
		{
			for (int l = num5; l <= num7; l++)
			{
				Tile tile2 = Main.tile[k, l];
				if (tile2.type >= 0 && !TileID.Sets.Boulders[tile2.type])
				{
					tile2.type = 1;
					if (tile2.wire())
					{
						tile2.actuator(actuator: true);
					}
				}
			}
		}
		WorldGen.PlaceTile(num, num2, 138);
		PlaceWireLine(position, 0, 1, yPush);
	}

```

**📎 ToolResult**

```
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
const W = 4200;
const g102t = new Uint16Array(readFileSync('/tmp/sw-slp/g/102_Water_Plants.typ').buffer.slice(0));
const g102a = new Uint8Array(readFileSync('/tmp/sw-slp/g/102_Water_Plants.act').buffer.slice(0));
const gf = readFileSync('/tmp/xxxx-fr/102_Water_Plants.fr');
const dv = new DataView(gf.buffer, gf.byteOffset, gf.byteLength);
const gcnt = dv.getUint32(0, true);
const gFrame = new Map();
for (let q = 0; q < gcnt; q++) {
  const off = 4 + q * 8;
  gFrame.set(dv.getUint32(off, true), [dv.getUint16(off + 4, true), dv.getUint16(off + 6, true)]);
}
const parts = new Uint32Array(readFileSync('/tmp/aaaaa-work/js101.fr').buffer.slice(0));
const cnt = parts[0];
const jt = new Uint32Array(readFileSync('/tmp/aaaaa-work/js101.frtyp').buffer.slice(0));
const jsFrame = new Map();
for (let q = 0; q < cnt; q++) {
  jsFrame.set(parts[1 + q * 3], [parts[2 + q * 3], parts[3 + q * 3]]);
}
let match = 0, diff = 0, vanOnly = 0, jsOnly = 0;
const diffs = [];
for (let y = 715; y <= 745; y++) {
  for (let x = 830; x <= 860; x++) {
    const i = y * W + x;
    const gIs = g102a[i] === 1 && g102t[i] === 314;
    const jIs = jt[i] === 314;
    if (gIs && jIs) {
      const g = gFrame.get(i), j = jsFrame.get(i);
      if (g && j && g[0] === j[0] && g[1] === j[1]) match++;
      else { diff++; if (diffs.length < 12) diffs.push(`(${x},${y}) g=${JSON.stringify(g)} j=${JSON.stringify(j)}`); }
    } else if (gIs) vanOnly++;
    else if (jIs) jsOnly++;
  }
}
console.log(`314 window x[830..860] y[715..745]: match=${match} diff=${diff} vanOnly=${vanOnly} jsOnly=${jsOnly}`);
diffs.forEach(d => console.log('  ' + d));

```

```
function makeTileDustRoll(ctx: SweepCtx, sheet: number, fx: number): void {
  if (!ctx.rng) return;
  const r = ctx.rng;
  switch (sheet) {
    case 2: case 477: case 27: case 243: case 244: case 204: case 478:
    case 641: case 370: case 171:
      r.nextInt(2);
      return;
    case 26: case 695:
      if (fx < 54) r.nextInt(2);
      return;
    case 583: case 584: case 585: case 586: case 587: case 588: case 589:
      r.nextInt(10);
      return;
    case 633:
      r.nextInt(6);
      return;
    case 634:
      r.nextInt(10);
      r.nextInt(12);
      return;
    case 238:
      r.nextInt(3);
      return;
    case 385:
      r.nextIntRange(68, 71);
      return;
    case 254:
      if (fx < 72) r.nextInt(3);
      else if (fx < 108) r.nextInt(2);
      else r.nextInt(4);
      return;
    default:
      if (FURNITURE_DUST.has(sheet)) r.nextInt(3);
  }
}
```

````
Now — the check2x2Sweep implementation (FinalCleanup flavor, ctx-based):

```ts
/** Check2x2（cs:48405-48807）——CCCCC 批补派发（cs:86396 case 411 / cs:86672 case 138）。
 *  锚点反解=本格减帧偏移（cs:48418-33；frameX≥36 修样式列 +36）；2×2 四格
 *  type+帧全等校验（内层 break，外层续跑——cs:48441-59）；138 巨石支=上方箱族
 *  豁免+底两格 SolidTileAllowBottomSlope（cs:48473-90）；411 走非巨石逐列
 *  地面门（cs:48460-71）。败→destroyObject 闸内整组 KillTile（尘掷走 killTileGen
 *  表）+尾 4×4 TileFrame(reset=false)（cs:48800-07）。掷=KillTile 尘掷表（138→0，
 *  411→10/格）。 */
const CHEST_PROTECT_2X2 = new Set([21, 467, 441, 468, 88, 470, 475]);   // cs:48483-85: BasicChest{21,467}+Fake{441,468}+88+470+475（HalfBrickPass UUUU 同源）
function check2x2Sweep(st: TileStore, ctx: SweepCtx, x: number, y: number, sheet: number): void {
  if (ctx.destroyObject) return;                                         // cs:48407
  const i0 = st.idx(x, y);
  let num2 = -Math.trunc(st.frameX[i0]! / 18);                           // cs:48418
  let num = 0;
  if (num2 < -1) { num2 += 2; num = 36; }
  num2 += x;
  let num4 = st.frameY[i0]!; let num5 = 0;
  while (num4 >= 36) { num4 -= 36; num5++; }                             // num3=36（非 172）
  const num6 = y - Math.trunc(num4 / 18);
  const isBoulder = BOULDER_SHEETS.has(sheet);                           // 138=true;411=false
  const inW = (k: number, l: number) => k >= 0 && l >= 0 && k < st.w && l < st.h;
  let flag = false;
  for (let k = num2; k < num2 + 2; k++) {
    for (let l = num6; l < num6 + 2; l++) {
      if (!inW(k, l)) { flag = true; break; }                            // GetTileSafely 新格→失配
      const q = st.idx(k, l);
      if (!st.flags[q] || SWEEP_SHEET[st.type[q]!] !== sheet
        || st.frameX[q] !== (k - num2) * 18 + num
        || st.frameY[q] !== (l - num6) * 18 + num5 * 36) { flag = true; break; }
    }
    if (sheet === 95 || sheet === 126) { /* 未派发 */ }
    else if (!isBoulder && sheet !== 132) {                              // cs:48460-71（411 支）
      const gi = inW(k, num6 + 2) ? st.idx(k, num6 + 2) : -1;
      if (gi < 0) flag = true;
      else {
        if (!st.flags[gi] || !(genSolidType(st.type[gi]!) || TILE_TABLE.has(SWEEP_SHEET[st.type[gi]!] ?? -1))) flag = true;
        if (st.half[gi]) flag = true;
      }
    }
  }
  if (isBoulder) {                                                       // cs:48473-90（138 支）
    const chestAbove = (cx: number, cy: number): boolean =>
      inW(cx, cy) && CHEST_PROTECT_2X2.has(SWEEP_SHEET[st.type[st.idx(cx, cy)]] ?? -1);
    if (!chestAbove(num2, num6 - 1) && !chestAbove(num2 + 1, num6 - 1)
      && !solidAllowBottom(st, num2, num6 + 2) && !solidAllowBottom(st, num2 + 1, num6 + 2)) flag = true;
  }
  if (!flag) return;
  ctx.destroyObject = true;                                              // cs:48567
  for (let k = num2; k < num2 + 2; k++) {
    for (let l = num6; l < num6 + 2; l++) {
      if (!inW(k, l)) continue;
      const q = st.idx(k, l);
      if (st.flags[q] && SWEEP_SHEET[st.type[q]!] === sheet) killTileGen(st, ctx, k, l);
    }
  }
  ctx.destroyObject = false;                                             // cs:48800
  for (let k = num2 - 1; k < num2 + 3; k++) {
    for (let l = num6 - 1; l < num6 + 3; l++) tileFrameGen(st, ctx, k, l, false);
  }
}
```
````

```
// AAAAA: compare vanilla span vs JS span; filter PASS headers AND '@'/'#' seg markers; normalize 'b 0 N'=='a N'
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
const norm = l => { const m = /^b 0 (\d+)$/.exec(l); return m ? 'a ' + m[1] : l; };
const load = p => readFileSync(p, 'utf8').split('\n').filter(l => l && !l.startsWith('PASS') && !l.startsWith('@') && !l.startsWith('#')).map(norm);
const van = load(process.argv[2]);
const js = load(process.argv[3]);
let d = 0;
const n = Math.min(van.length, js.length);
for (; d < n; d++) if (van[d] !== js[d]) break;
console.log(`van=${van.length} js=${js.length} firstDiff=${d} (van '${van[d]}' vs js '${js[d]}') tailVan=${van.length - d} tailJs=${js.length - d}`);

```

```
## CCCCC 交付报告

### ⓪ 根因：两件移交诊断均部分错位，实证重定罪（vanilla 行号+JS 行号）

**件 1（411 起爆器）真根因 ≠ findDown。** `Searches.Down`（Searches.cs:60-71）i 从 0 起含起点行——JS `findDown`（MicroBiomesPass.ts:700-705 旧）镜像无差，两侧 detGround 同=736。真根因在落块锚：vanilla `PlaceTile(x,y,411)`（WorldGen.cs:59766-59769）→ `Place2x2`（cs:51352-51445）在 **cs:51431 `x--; y--;` 后写 (x-1..x)×(y-1..y)，右下锚**，且带双门（cs:51371-51405：逐列 2×2 四格空置门 + 地面门 `nactive&&(SolidTile2||tileTable)`），门败**静默整写放弃**（cs:73-75 不接返回值，WireLine/保护图照跑仍 return true）。JS 曾按左上锚**无条件**写 (detX..detX+1)×(detTop..detTop+1)=低一格右一列。金标全图 411 位扫描证明 vanilla 在 (861,735) 段**本就未落块**（(856,735) 活性黏土占格空置门败）——JS 的幽灵块才是差异（AAAAA"轨道穿杀"系误读）。

**件 2（3 格 18,18vs0,0）真根因 ≠ 引擎缺派发。** 三格 = DeadMansChest 三块巨石的底右格：`ActuallyPlaceBoulderTrap` 的 `PlaceTile(num,num2,138)`（DeadMansChestBiome.cs:607）同走 Place2x2 右下锚 → vanilla 落 (cx-1..cx)×(cy-1..cy)；JS 左上锚写 = 整块 **(+1,+1) 偏移**（八通道+.fr 双重实证）。引擎 case 138 派发本身确缺（真缺口，同补）——但 Check2x2 不写帧，单补派发无法收敛该 3 格。

### ① 修复（diff 三文件域内）
```
